Processing data

3 Binning five Gradients cruises

First, we will carry out the actual binning.

## Focus on the Gradients cruises
cruises = c("SCOPE_16", ## G1
            "MGL1704", ## G2
            "KM1906", ## G3
            "TN397_740", ## G4
            "TN412_130") ## G5

for(cruisename in cruises){
  print(cruisename)

  ## Read particle-level data.
  vct_dir <- str_glue("{raw_data_dir}/{cruisename}/{cruisename}_vct_slim")
  vct_files <- list.files(vct_dir, "\\.parquet$", full.names = TRUE)
  list_of_df = lapply(1:length(vct_files), function(ifile){ read_parquet(vct_files[ifile]) })
  vct <- bind_rows(list_of_df)
  invisible(gc())
  
  ## Do the actual gridding now
  vct["fsc_small_coord"] <- as.integer(cut(vct[["fsc_small"]],
                                           grid_bins[["fsc_small"]],
                                           labels = FALSE,
                                           right = FALSE))
  vct["pe_coord"] <- as.integer(cut(vct[["pe"]],
                                    grid_bins[["pe"]],
                                    labels = FALSE,
                                    right = FALSE))
  vct["chl_small_coord"] <- as.integer(cut(vct[["chl_small"]],
                                           grid_bins[["chl_small"]],
                                           labels = FALSE,
                                           right = FALSE))

  ## Remove beads
  vct = vct %>% filter(pop != "beads")
  
  ## Gridding the data and summing the Qcs in each 3d grid bin.
  gridded <- vct %>%
    group_by(date, across(ends_with("_coord")), pop) %>%
    summarise(Qc = sum(Qc), .groups="drop") %>%
    select(date, fsc_small_coord, chl_small_coord, pe_coord,  Qc) %>%
    arrange(date, fsc_small_coord, chl_small_coord, pe_coord)

  ## Aggregate to hourly level
  hourly_gridded <- gridded %>%
    group_by(date=floor_date(date, "1 hours"), across(ends_with("_coord"))) %>%
    summarise(Qc=sum(Qc), .groups="drop") %>%
    arrange(date, fsc_small_coord, chl_small_coord, pe_coord)
  invisible(gc())
  
  ## Each cruise's binned data is now saved to a file
  filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-without-cleaning.csv")
  outfile = file.path(outputdir, filename)
  write.csv(hourly_gridded, file = outfile, row.names = FALSE)
  cat("wrote to", outfile, fill=TRUE)
}

Let’s see, for every cruise, 1d plots for all 3 dimensions.

cruises = c("SCOPE_16", ## G1
            "MGL1704", ## G2
            "KM1906", ## G3
            "TN397_740", ## G4
            "TN412_130") ## G5

for(cruisename in cruises){
  print(cruisename)

  ## Load data
  csv_filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-without-cleaning.csv")
  datlist <- csv_to_datlist(outputdir = outputdir,
                            csv_filename = csv_filename)
  ylist <- datlist$ylist
  biomass_list <- datlist$biomass_list
  dates <- datlist$datetimes
  rm(datlist)
  gc()
  
  plotlist = lapply(1:3, function(idim){
  datobj_1d = flowmix::collapse_3d_to_1d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, idim = idim)
  flowtrend::plot_1d(ylist = datobj_1d$ylist,
                     countslist = datobj_1d$countslist,  bin = TRUE,
                     x = dates) +
    scale_fill_gradientn(colours = c("white", "black", "yellow", "red")) +
    ggtitle(cruisename) +
    ylab(c("fsc (forward scatter)", "chl", "pe")[idim])
  })
  cowplot::plot_grid(plotlist = plotlist, ncol = 1) %>% print()
}

3.1 Data cleaning notes

Here are some points that came up while cleaning data.

  1. Gradients 3 has small-diameter particles in many time points that need to be manually removed. We’ll do this by taking the upper-most bins in diameter and removing them altogether.
  2. We use a bigger number (80) for the number of bins in fsc because, across all cruises, fsc values are more widely variable.
  3. fsc stands for forward scatter, which can be converted (using MIE theory) to diameter, using Francois Ribalet’s algorithm.
  4. G5 (TN412_130) PE values all fall in the bottom bin. Although at first glance it may look like censored points, they aren’t! We don’t and shouldn’t remove these!
  5. TODO: G5 (TN412_130) requires a bit more work. Some cytograms need to be visualized and removed manually.

3.2 Cleaning Gradients 3

## Warning: Raster pixels are placed at uneven horizontal intervals and will be shifted
## ℹ Consider using `geom_tile()` instead.
## Raster pixels are placed at uneven horizontal intervals and will be shifted
## ℹ Consider using `geom_tile()` instead.
## Find the cutoff's bin number (in fsc_small)
cutoffs = list()
new_countslist = list()
april_16 = which(lubridate::day(dates) == 16)
april_17 = which(lubridate::day(dates) == 17)
april_18 = which(lubridate::day(dates) == 18)
TT = length(biomass_list)
for(tt in 1:TT){

  one_count = datobj$countslist[[tt]]
  one_y = datobj$ylist[[tt]] 

  ## Find cutoff point
  diffs = one_count  %>% diff()
  a = which(sapply(1:(length(diffs)-1), function(ii){
    (sign(diffs[ii]) != sign(diffs[ii+1])) &
      (diff(diffs)[ii]>0)
  }))
  a = min(a, na.rm = TRUE)
  if(tt %in% c(april_16, april_17, april_18)){
    a = a[a<=9] 
  } else {
    a = a[a<=5] 
  }
  stopifnot(length(a) <= 1)
  cutoffs[[tt]] = one_y[a]
}

## Next, removing the points with small fsc_small altogether.
## (Also remove the top-most row in fsc_small and chl_small.)
new_biomass_list = biomass_list
new_ylist = ylist
for(tt in 1:TT){
  one_count = biomass_list[[tt]]
  one_y = ylist[[tt]]
  if( length(cutoffs[[tt]]) > 0 ){
    irow_to_remove1 = which(one_y[,"fsc_small_coord"] <= cutoffs[[tt]])
  } else {
    irow_to_remove1 = c()
  }

  ## Removing the top rows
  irow_to_remove2 = which(one_y[,"fsc_small_coord"] == 80)
  irow_to_remove3 = which(one_y[,"chl_small_coord"] == 40)
  irow_to_remove = c(irow_to_remove1, irow_to_remove2, irow_to_remove3)
  if(length(irow_to_remove) > 0){
  new_ylist[[tt]] = one_y[-irow_to_remove,]
  new_biomass_list[[tt]] = one_count[-irow_to_remove]
  }
}

## Remove these odd time points

## "2019-04-16 17:00:00 UTC"

new_ylist = new_ylist[-c(21, 136:139)]
new_biomass_list = new_biomass_list[-c(21, 136:139)]
stopifnot(length(new_dates)==length(new_ylist))

  colnames(coords_tibble) <- c("fsc_small_coord", "chl_small_coord", "pe_coord") # Rename columns

})

3.3 Cleaning other Gradients cruises

Use a similar pipeline: (1) remove edge points in fsc and chl, (2) visually remove weird time points.

cruises = c("SCOPE_16", ## G1
            "MGL1704", ## G2
            ## "KM1906", ## G3 ## Already done in the above code block
            "TN397_740", ## G4
            "TN412_130") ## G5
for(cruisename in cruises){

  ## Read in a data file
  csv_filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-without-cleaning.csv")
  datlist = csv_to_datlist("~/repos/process-data/output/seaflow",
                           csv_filename)
  ylist = datlist$ylist
  biomass_list = datlist$biomass_list
  dates = datlist$datetimes

  
  ## Collapse into 1d for plotting
  datobj = flowmix::collapse_3d_to_1d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, idim = 1)
  
  ## Plotting before processing
  gg_before = my_plotter(datobj$ylist, datobj$countslist, dates, paste0(cruisename, " before any processing"))
  gg_before
  
  ## For G2 G3 and G4 (but not G1) remove *top* fsc_small_coord bin altogether
  new_biomass_list = biomass_list
  new_ylist = ylist
  new_dates = dates
    TT = length(ylist)
    for(tt in 1:TT){
      one_count = biomass_list[[tt]]
      one_y = ylist[[tt]]

      ## Remove top row of FSC
      irow_to_remove2 = which(one_y[,"fsc_small_coord"] == 80)

      ## Remove top row of CHL
      irow_to_remove1 = c()
      if(cruisename == "TN412_130"){
        ## irow_to_remove1 = c()
        irow_to_remove1 = which(one_y[,"chl_small_coord"] == 40)
      } else if (cruisename == "SCOPE_16"){
        irow_to_remove1 = which(one_y[,"chl_small_coord"] >= 39)
      } else {
        irow_to_remove1 = which(one_y[,"chl_small_coord"] == 40)
      }

      irow_to_remove = unique(c(irow_to_remove1, irow_to_remove2))##, irow_to_remove3))
      if(length(irow_to_remove) > 0){
        new_ylist[[tt]] = one_y[-irow_to_remove,]
        new_biomass_list[[tt]] = one_count[-irow_to_remove]
      }
    }
  
  ## Plotting after processing
  datobj = flowmix::collapse_3d_to_1d(ylist = new_ylist, countslist = new_biomass_list, idim = 1)
  gg_after = my_plotter(datobj$ylist, datobj$countslist, dates, paste0(cruisename, " after processing"))
  gg_after
  gg_combined = cowplot::plot_grid(plotlist = list(gg_before, gg_after), ncol = 1, labels = NULL)
  plot(gg_combined)
  gg_combined %>% 
    ggsave(filename =  file.path(outputdir, paste0(cruisename, "-processing.png")), width = 10, height = 13)


  ## Combine these things back into a csv-saveable table.
  dates_list <- new_dates %>% as.list()
  recombined_list <- purrr::pmap(list(dates_list, new_ylist, new_biomass_list), function(date_str, coords_matrix, qc_vector) {
    coords_tibble <- as_tibble(coords_matrix, .name_repair = "unique")
    colnames(coords_tibble) <- c("fsc_small_coord", "chl_small_coord", "pe_coord") # Rename columns
    coords_tibble %>%
      mutate(date = date_str, Qc = qc_vector)
  })
  hourly_gridded_reconstructed <- bind_rows(recombined_list) %>%
    select(date, fsc_small_coord, chl_small_coord, pe_coord, Qc)
  
  ## Each cruise is now saved to a file
  filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-after-cleaning.csv")
  outfile = file.path(outputdir, filename)
  write.csv(hourly_gridded_reconstructed, file = outfile, row.names = FALSE)
  cat("wrote to", outfile, fill=TRUE)
}

## Warning: Raster pixels are placed at uneven horizontal intervals and will be shifted

## Raster pixels are placed at uneven horizontal intervals and will be shifted
## ℹ Consider using `geom_tile()` instead.

## wrote to output/seaflow/TN412_130-gridded-after-cleaning.csv

Let’s see all 1d plots AFTER the data cleaning.

cruises = c("SCOPE_16", ## G1
            "MGL1704", ## G2
            "KM1906", ## G3
            "TN397_740", ## G4
            "TN412_130") ## G5

for(cruisename in cruises){
  print(cruisename)

  ## Load data
  csv_filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-without-cleaning.csv")
  datlist <- csv_to_datlist(outputdir = outputdir,
                            csv_filename = csv_filename)
  ylist <- datlist$ylist
  biomass_list <- datlist$biomass_list
  dates <- datlist$datetimes
  rm(datlist)
  gc()
  
  before_plotlist = lapply(1:3, function(idim){
  datobj_1d = flowmix::collapse_3d_to_1d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, idim = idim)
  flowtrend::plot_1d(ylist = datobj_1d$ylist,
                     ## countslist = datobj_1d$countslist,
                     countslist = datobj_1d$countslist %>% sapply(function(a)a/sum(a)),
                     bin = TRUE,
                     x = dates) +
    scale_fill_gradientn(colours = c("white", "black", "yellow", "red")) +
    ggtitle(cruisename) +
    ylab(c("fsc (forward scatter)", "chl", "pe")[idim])
  })


  ## Load data
  csv_filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-after-cleaning.csv")
  datlist <- csv_to_datlist(outputdir = outputdir,
                            csv_filename = csv_filename)
  ylist <- datlist$ylist
  biomass_list <- datlist$biomass_list
  dates <- datlist$datetimes
  rm(datlist)
  gc()
  
  after_plotlist = lapply(1:3, function(idim){
  datobj_1d = flowmix::collapse_3d_to_1d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, idim = idim)
  flowtrend::plot_1d(ylist = datobj_1d$ylist,
                     ## countslist = datobj_1d$countslist,  bin = TRUE,
                     countslist = datobj_1d$countslist %>% sapply(function(a)a/sum(a)),
                     bin=TRUE,
                     x = dates) +
    scale_fill_gradientn(colours = c("white", "black", "yellow", "red")) +
    ggtitle(cruisename) +
    ylab(c("fsc (forward scatter)", "chl", "pe")[idim])
  })

  ## cowplot::plot_grid(plotlist = before_plotlist, ncol = 1),
  cowplot::plot_grid(plotlist = c(before_plotlist, after_plotlist), ncol = 2, byrow = FALSE)  %>% ## print() %>% 
    ggsave(filename =  file.path(outputdir, paste0(cruisename, "-after-processing.png")), width = 40, height = 15) 
}

3.4 Code to visualize entire 3d cytograms.

## Take one cruise
cruisename = "TN412_130"
csv_filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-without-cleaning.csv")
datlist <- csv_to_datlist(outputdir = outputdir,
                          csv_filename = csv_filename)
pre_ylist <- datlist$ylist
pre_biomass_list <- datlist$biomass_list
pre_dates <- datlist$datetimes
rm(datlist)
gc()
##             used   (Mb) gc trigger    (Mb) limit (Mb)   max used    (Mb)
## Ncells  10212853  545.5   20601708  1100.3         NA   80225458  4284.6
## Vcells 624762780 4766.6 1971483821 15041.3     102400 2127555167 16232.0
## The same cruise after cleaning
cruisename = "TN412_130"
csv_filename = paste0(cruisename, "-gridded-after-cleaning.csv")
datlist <- csv_to_datlist(outputdir = outputdir,
                          csv_filename = csv_filename)
ylist <- datlist$ylist
biomass_list <- datlist$biomass_list
dates <- datlist$datetimes
rm(datlist)
gc()
##             used   (Mb) gc trigger    (Mb) limit (Mb)   max used    (Mb)
## Ncells  10212908  545.5   20601708  1100.3         NA   80225458  4284.6
## Vcells 624763412 4766.6 1971483821 15041.3     102400 2127555167 16232.0
  cowplot::plot_grid(plotlist = 
                       list(flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = pre_ylist, countslist = pre_biomass_list, tt = 510),
                            flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = pre_ylist, countslist = pre_biomass_list, tt = 511),
                            flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = pre_ylist, countslist = pre_biomass_list, tt = 512),
                            flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = pre_ylist, countslist = pre_biomass_list, tt = 513),
                            flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, tt = 510),
                            flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, tt = 511),
                            flowtrend::plot_3d(ylist = ylist, countslist = biomass_list, tt = 512),
